base = PosixPath('/tmp/pytest-of-usain/pytest-922/popen-gw12/w2-02-close0')

    def closed_ticket(base):
        """A project whose one ticket is closed by ``gov close``: ``(project, close record path, its text)``.
    
        The project holds what a close needs and nothing else: a path map with two workers, the kernel's schema files,
        one check declaration of its own (``true``), the kernel's ticket tool, the source the ticket names, the ticket
        in progress, a passing acceptance test, an engineer's commit with ``Task:``, ``Role:`` and ``Implements:``, a
        checkpoint and a loaded record store.
        """
        project = Project(base)
        ticket, wbs = CLOSE_TICKET, CLOSE_WBS
        project.write(".gitignore", ".gov-runtime/\n")
        project.write("governance/project/path-map.yaml", "namespaces: {}\nclose_workers: 2\n")
        project.kernel_schemas()
        project.write("template/governance/kernel/checks/trivial.yaml",
                      'id: "trivial"\nfamily: "schema/invariants"\ntier: "G1"\nseverity: "hard-block"\ncommand: "true"\n')
        tk = REPO_ROOT / "template/governance/kernel/bin/tk"
        project.write("governance/kernel/bin/tk", tk.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
        (project.root / "governance/kernel/bin/tk").chmod(0o755)
        project.write("docs/adr/DEC-000.md", markdown(shared("DEC-000", "decision", title="The base decision")))
        _commit_as(project, "initial project", ())
        project.write(f".tickets/{ticket}.md", _TICKET.format(ticket=ticket, wbs=wbs))
        _commit_as(project, "ticket", ("Role: orchestrator",))
        project.write(f"tests/acceptance/{wbs}/README.md", f"# {wbs}\n")
        project.write(f"tests/acceptance/{wbs}/test_pass.py", "def test_pass():\n    assert True\n")
        _commit_as(project, "acceptance tests", (f"Task: {ticket}", "Role: independent-test-designer",
                                                 "Implements: DEC-000"))
        project.write("src/example/feature.py", "# feature\n")
        _commit_as(project, "implement", (f"Task: {ticket}", "Role: engineer", "Implements: DEC-000"))
        done = gov(project, "checkpoint", "--ticket", ticket, "--trigger", "stop", "--next", "resume", "--json")
        assert done.returncode == 0, f"the fixture's gov checkpoint failed:\n{done.stdout}\n{done.stderr}"
        _commit_as(project, "checkpoint", ("Role: orchestrator",))
        done = subprocess.run([sys.executable, "-c", "import sys\nfrom gov.store import load\nload(sys.argv[1])\n",
                               str(project.root)], cwd=str(project.root), env=env(project.home), capture_output=True,
                              text=True, timeout=TIMEOUT_S, stdin=subprocess.DEVNULL)

    def test_the_command_set_is_the_twelve_and_the_three_recorded_ones(empty_project, sandbox):
        """Rewritten (DEC-542) from ``test_this_ticket_adds_no_command``, which pinned the three as found. Every
        command ``gov`` offers is in the command list, every ``gov`` command of the list is offered, and the
        recorded module command is not among them.
    
        Rewritten again in W2-16's last round (DEC-761, a recorded rewrite, DEC-491). Reason: ``gov --help`` offers
        ``release`` (W2-16), which DEC-753 records in the command lists only after W2-20 closes, so the case failed
        on a command the owner has approved and whose recording is scheduled. ``release``, and only ``release``, is
        excused while the lists lack it (DEC-753); the case passes unchanged once they record it. The purpose is
        kept: any other command ``gov`` offers that the lists do not record still fails it, a recorded command gone
        from ``gov --help`` still fails it, and the module command is still none of ``gov``'s.
    
        Revised for DEC-970 (W2-96, P-W2-96-CMDLIST (a); a recorded rewrite, DEC-959 item 6): ``accept`` is recorded,
        so the recorded ``gov`` commands are four, not the three of the case's name, which W1-32's README map names
        and so stays as it is.
    
        Revised for W2-20 (DEC-491; ported for DEC-1097 (8) from 173e8e97a): W2-20's KPI adds ``resources`` to the
        recorded ``gov`` commands; the set is still exact, and ``release`` is still excused until recorded (DEC-753).
    
        Revised for D-10's red fix (W2-20, DAEO-r30l; DEC-753, a recorded rewrite by a fresh test designer, DEC-491):
        ``gov --help`` offers ``brief`` (W2-152, DEC-1088 (3)), ``links`` (W2-129, DEC-1074 (7)) and ``plan``
        (W2-136, DEC-1088), approved commands the lists did not record, so the case failed on them. W1-07's lines
        record them; the pinned set is still exact, any other unrecorded command still fails, a recorded command
        gone from ``gov --help`` still fails, the module command is still none of ``gov``'s, and ``release`` alone
        is still excused until recorded (DEC-753).
        """
        assert set(RECORDED) - set(EXCUSED_UNTIL_RECORDED) == {"accept", "brief", "ci", "launch", "links", "plan",
                                                                "resources", "telemetry"}, RECORDED

        run = support.gov(empty_project, sandbox, "--help")
        commands = _commands(run)
        assert set(cli_support.RESERVED_COMMANDS) <= set(commands), f"a Wave 1 operation is gone\n{run.describe()}"
        gone = sorted(set(RECORDED) - set(commands))
        assert not gone, f"a recorded command is gone from gov --help: {gone}\n{run.describe()}"
        extra = sorted(set(commands) - set(cli_support.RESERVED_COMMANDS) - set(RECORDED) - set(EXCUSED_UNTIL_RECORDED))

    def test_a_request_past_its_time_limit_does_not_hold_the_search(boxes):
        # Rewrite-Reason: DEC-1048 (5) (W2-145's test designer): the search past its time limit used to fall back to a
        # private reranker that scored it; there is none now: it goes on unranked, named, within the limit plus 1 s.
        limit = 3.0
        box = boxes(settings={support.TIMEOUT_SETTING: limit})
        service = box.warm()
        box.for_pid(service, predict_delay=60)  # the service's model now takes a minute to score
        query, given = query_of("slow"), candidates("slow", 5)
        report = box.search(query, given)
        assert raised(report) is None, f"a search whose request ran past its time limit raised: {raised(report)}"
>       assert report["seconds"] <= limit + 1.0, \
            (f"a search whose request ran past its time limit ({support.TIMEOUT_SETTING}={limit:g}) took "
             f"{report['seconds']:.1f} s: within the limit plus 1 s (W2-145 S4)")

    def evidence_of(run):
        """The evidence block of a close: ``result.evidence`` of a passed close (and the same block in its close
        record), ``error.details.evidence`` of a refused one."""
        envelope = envelope_of(run)
        if envelope.get("ok") is True:
            holder, where = envelope.get("result") or {}, "the result"
        else:
            holder, where = (envelope.get("error") or {}).get("details") or {}, "the refusal's details"
        evidence = holder.get(EVIDENCE_KEY)

    def evidence_of(run):
        """The evidence block of a close: ``result.evidence`` of a passed close (and the same block in its close
        record), ``error.details.evidence`` of a refused one."""
        envelope = envelope_of(run)
        if envelope.get("ok") is True:
            holder, where = envelope.get("result") or {}, "the result"
        else:

    def evidence_of(run):
        """The evidence block of a close: ``result.evidence`` of a passed close (and the same block in its close
        record), ``error.details.evidence`` of a refused one."""
        envelope = envelope_of(run)
        if envelope.get("ok") is True:
            holder, where = envelope.get("result") or {}, "the result"
        else:

    def evidence_of(run):

    def evidence_of(run):
        """The evidence block of a close: ``result.evidence`` of a passed close (and the same block in its close
        record), ``error.details.evidence`` of a refused one."""
        envelope = envelope_of(run)
        if envelope.get("ok") is True:
            holder, where = envelope.get("result") or {}, "the result"
        else:
            holder, where = (envelope.get("error") or {}).get("details") or {}, "the refusal's details"
        evidence = holder.get(EVIDENCE_KEY)
